HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_059457e54a5eefdad375","text":"4. **The personal giving is also service-care toward the addressee.** Lexical evidence: ع ط و B003, “serving by attending and handing things”; ر ح م B001, mercy and tenderness; ر ب ب B002, repair, nurture, and completion. Structural cue: the direct object suffix in “أعطيناك” places the addressee as the one attended to; the basmalah’s mercy precedes the grant. Assigned role: ع ط و B003 makes giving intimate and service-like; ر ح م and ر ب ب make that service nurturing rather than transactional. Abductive move: I infer from personal address plus mercy/nurture branches that the gift is care administered to the addressee. Reading change: the ayah reads not only as royal bestowal from above, but as attentive provision that repairs and completes the recipient’s situation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002","root_001286/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0bc1ca824560c3a1e2d6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:1:3\",\"root_001286\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bolluk, yalnızca miktar bildirmez; düşmanca üstünlük yarışında muhataba yakıştırılan eksikliği tersine çeviren bir çokluk olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002","root_001286/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0c6e811f54fb7133290b","text":"108:2 changed the gift most: the فـ before “pray” made ع ط و B002 and ك ث ر B001 read as a trust that seeks return, not a terminal possession. Then 108:3 sharpened ك ث ر B002, the rivalry/outnumbering branch, because the hater’s “abtar” condition made abundance answer a hostile calculus of lack. A more material surprise also emerged from 108:2: ن ح ر B009, cloud pouring itself out, and ر ب ب B013, abundant collected water, made “kawthar” feel less like a static object and more like an overflowing ecology.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002","root_001286/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1f7d4f371162d4c3e068","text":"1. **The gift is a handed-over trust that generates response, not inert abundance.** Lexical evidence: ع ط و B002, “handing over and giving”; ك ث ر B001, “abundance and numerical increase”; later ص ل و B003, prescribed worship, and ر ب ب B001/B002, lordship plus nurture/completion. Structural cue: divine “we gave you” is followed immediately by “so pray to your Rabb.” Assigned role: ع ط و supplies transfer; ك ث ر supplies magnitude; ص ل و and ر ب ب supply the expected return of the received thing toward its owner/nurturer. Abductive move: I supply the causal arrow that a bestowed abundance creates a ritual obligation of directed gratitude. Reading change: “al-kawthar” reads as entrusted superabundance whose first visible fruit is worship, not as a possession that stops with the recipient.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002","root_001286/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_63e47f1c87b134c5a6dc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:1:2\",\"root_001028\",[\"B002\"]],[\"108:1:3\",\"root_001286\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Verilen bolluk, alıcıda duran bir mülkten çok, namaz ve kurbanla karşılık verilmesini bekleyen bir emanet ve dolaşım başlangıcı olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6a75854a492ec1f100ea","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:1:3\",\"root_001286\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bolluk, elde duran bir birikimden çok yükselip taşan ve dışarı akmaya yönelen hareketli bir fazlalık niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7aca93f93e1d10ece663","text":"1. **A gift that initiates return rather than ending in possession.** Fixed `أَعْطَيْنَٰكَ`, root `ع ط و`, `B002`, supplies handing over, and fixed `ٱلْكَوْثَرَ`, root `ك ث ر`, `B001`, supplies extreme abundance. The next ayah begins with consequential `فَ` and coordinates prayer (`ص ل و`, `B003`) with sacrifice (`ن ح ر`, `B002`). Giving and abundance form the received resource; the two imperatives form its ritual and material return. The packet supplies this sequence and the connective, while I infer the directional mechanism abundant gift -> responsive worship and expenditure. The fixed ayah thus reads as the opening of circulation and obligation, not an inert transfer to be retained.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_91482180c66b5e980727","text":"3. **Abundance that overturns hostile comparison.** Fixed `ٱلْكَوْثَرَ` activates root `ك ث ر`, `B002`, rivalry, outnumbering, and superiority through abundance, alongside direct `B001` manyness. The rival field becomes visible only with `شَانِئَكَ` (`ش ن ء`, `B001`, enemy/hatred) and `ٱلْأَبْتَرُ` (`ب ت ر`, `B002`, cut-off posterity, mention, or good) in `108:3`. `B002` assigns the gift a comparative role: it is abundance whose force appears against an antagonist's lack of continuation. The packet supplies abundance, enemy, and cutoff; I infer that the final predication resolves the rivalry latent in `ك ث ر`. The ayah consequently reads as a reversal of hostile social arithmetic, not merely a statement that the gift is large.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_91e7636fc3434e0a28f0","text":"4. **Exploratory: abundance as a surge rather than a stock.** Fixed root `ك ث ر`, `B005`, names `كوثر` as roiling, rising dust and also covers becoming extremely abundant. Its motion is activated by the packet's neighboring material images: root `س م و`, `B004`, includes cloud and rain, while root `ن ح ر`, `B009`, includes a cloud pouring out abundant water. The branch's role is to make `ٱلْكَوْثَرَ` dynamically expanding and discharging. The packet contains no explicit dust, cloud, or water noun here; I infer the linkage from proximity, repeated abundance, and the movement from `ٱلْكَوْثَرَ` to `وَٱنْحَرْ`, so this is the weak step. On this activation, the gift feels like a surging plenitude that anticipates outflow, rather than a static quantity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9c50766db960aef78447","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:1:3\",\"root_001286\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Bolluk, sayısal çokluğun ötesinde yeni hayat üreten verimli bir çoğalma olarak da okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B005","root_001286/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a968455d13d21de11fed","text":"3. **“Kawthar” takes on a roiling, life-bearing materiality.** Lexical evidence: ك ث ر B005, “roiling abundant dust”; ك ث ر B006, palm pith or inflorescence; ر ب ب B008/B013, layered cloud and abundant collected water; ن ح ر B009, cloud pouring itself out with water; س م و B004, overhead sky, cloud, rain, vegetation. Structural cue: the packet’s abundance word is surrounded by sky/rain/cloud branches in the basmalah’s س م و and the command’s ر ب ب/ن ح ر inventories. Assigned role: ك ث ر gives excessive swelling/rising; sky/cloud/water branches turn that excess into an ecological cycle; palm pith/inflorescence gives a plant-life image. Abductive move: I supply the arrow that a very abundant gift may be imagined as a swelling natural flow that nourishes, not only as countable quantity. Reading change: “al-kawthar” becomes dynamically fertile abundance, like cloudburst, rising dust, and plant interior, rather than an abstract numeral.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_af4ca7b80d498ff43d2a","text":"2. **Giving as attentive care.** The surface verb is directly covered by `ع ط و`, `B002`, but `108:2` activates dormant `ع ط و`, `B003`, serving someone by attending to a need, through `رَبِّكَ` and root `ر ب ب`, `B002`, nurturing and administering stage by stage. The first-person giver, second-person recipient, and following possessive lord-title make the transfer relational; `B003` gives it the role of personally fitted provision. The packet does not state a particular need or explicitly identify the giver with `رَبِّكَ`; those are the abductive links I supply to explain the person-to-person grammar and sequence. `أَعْطَيْنَٰكَ` then reads not only as conveyance but as an act of care that takes charge of the recipient's condition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b362ffcdb2eb7a332f1a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:1:2\",\"root_001028\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kişiye doğrudan veriş, yalnızca bir aktarım değil, onun durumunu gözetip tamamlamaya yönelen özenli bir bakım niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002","root_001286/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c1e09d30bb58b779a785","text":"2. **Abundance reverses a contest over number, status, and lasting trace.** Lexical evidence: ك ث ر B002, “outnumbering and rivalry in abundance”; ك ث ر B003, abundance attached to a person, speech, or claims; س م و B007/B008, rivaling upward and good repute; final ب ت ر B002, cut-off progeny, renown, and good. Structural cue: the middle grant of “kawthar” faces the final exclusive judgment “he is the abtar.” Assigned role: ك ث ر supplies the surplus side of a rivalry; ب ت ر supplies the failed rival’s lack; س م و supplies the field of public elevation/repute. Abductive move: I infer a social contest in which the hater’s attempt to define the addressee as cut short is answered by divine over-abundance. Reading change: the ayah reads as a reversal of hostile accounting: the addressee’s share is not merely “much,” but much in the exact arena where the hater imagines lack.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f15f4cd38f4e4d901ab7","text":"Later `108:2` corrected the first-pass tendency to let the gift terminate in possession: consequential `فَصَلِّ` and sacrificial `وَٱنْحَرْ` made `ع ط و`, `B002`, and `ك ث ر`, `B001`, the input to a return mechanism.
